000690 | def detect_proxy(home):
000691 |     directory = home/'Library/Application Support/io.github.clash-verge-rev.clash-verge-rev'
000692 |     if directory.is_dir():
000693 |         group = 'GLOBAL'
000694 |         config = directory/'clash-verge.yaml'
000695 |         if config.exists():
000696 |             data, _ = small_file(config)
000697 |             text = data.decode('utf-8')
000698 |             # Read the first block-style select group, without evaluating YAML.
000699 |             section = re.search(r'(?ms)^proxy-groups:\s*\n(.*?)(?=^[^\s#-][^\n:]*:|\Z)', text)
000700 |             if section:
000701 |                 for block in re.split(r'(?m)^\s*-\s+', section[1]):
000702 |                     name = re.search(r'(?m)^\s*name:\s*(.*?)\s*$', block)
000703 |                     kind = re.search(r'(?m)^\s*type:\s*[\'"]?select[\'"]?\s*$', block)
000704 |                     if name and kind:
000705 |                         raw = name[1]
000706 |                         group = shlex.split(raw, comments=True)[0] if raw.startswith(('"', "'")) else raw.split(' #',1)[0]
000707 |                         break
000708 |         return dict(PROXY_MODE='clash-verge', PROXY_GROUP=group)
000709 |     vpn = run('/usr/sbin/scutil', '--nc', 'list', check=False).stdout
000710 |     return dict(PROXY_MODE='shadowrocket' if 'com.liguangming.Shadowrocket' in vpn else 'auto',
000711 |                 PROXY_GROUP='GLOBAL')

000714 | def installation_settings(owner, entry):
000715 |     home = Path(pwd.getpwuid(owner).pw_dir)
000716 |     values = dict(entry['literals'])
000717 |     with as_owner(owner) if os.geteuid() == 0 else contextlib.nullcontext():
000718 |         config = home/'.claude-switcher/v2/config.json'
000719 |         if config.exists():
000720 |             data, _ = small_file(config)
000721 |             current = json.loads(data)
000722 |             for key, field in [('COUNTRY_TARGET','country_target'), ('TZ_TARGET','timezone_target'),
000723 |                                ('LOCALE_TARGET','locale_target'), ('PROXY_MODE','proxy_mode')]:
000724 |                 if current.get(field) is not None:
000725 |                     values[key] = current[field]
000726 |         if not all(values.get(key) for key in ('COUNTRY_TARGET','TZ_TARGET')):
000727 |             detected = detect_region()
000728 |             if values.get('COUNTRY_TARGET') and values['COUNTRY_TARGET'] != detected['COUNTRY_TARGET']:
000729 |                 raise ValueError('已有目标国家与当前出口不一致；请先确认地区配置')
000730 |             for key, value in detected.items():
000731 |                 if not values.get(key):
000732 |                     values[key] = value
000733 |         values.setdefault('LOCALE_TARGET', LOCALES.get(values['COUNTRY_TARGET'], 'en_US'))
000734 |         if not values.get('PROXY_MODE'):
000735 |             for key, value in detect_proxy(home).items():
000736 |                 values.setdefault(key, value)
000737 |         values.setdefault('PROXY_GROUP', 'GLOBAL')
000738 |     if not isinstance(values['COUNTRY_TARGET'], str) or not re.fullmatch('[A-Z]{2}', values['COUNTRY_TARGET']):
000739 |         raise ValueError('configured country target is invalid')
000740 |     if not isinstance(values['LOCALE_TARGET'], str) or not re.fullmatch('[A-Za-z0-9_@.-]{1,80}', values['LOCALE_TARGET']):
000741 |         raise ValueError('configured locale target is invalid')
000742 |     if any(not isinstance(value,str) or any(ord(c)<32 for c in value) for value in values.values()):
000743 |         raise ValueError('invalid installation configuration literal')
000744 |     validate_timezone_target(values['TZ_TARGET'])
000745 |     validate_proxy_mode(values['PROXY_MODE'])
000746 |     return values

000749 | def read_timezone_target(owner):
000750 |     home = Path(pwd.getpwuid(owner).pw_dir)
000751 |     # Read owner-controlled data as that owner, never execute or source it.
000752 |     with as_owner(owner) if os.geteuid() == 0 else contextlib.nullcontext():
000753 |         config = home/'.claude-switcher/v2/config.json'
000754 |         if config.exists():
000755 |             data, _ = small_file(config)
000756 |             target = json.loads(data).get('timezone_target')
000757 |         else:
000758 |             data, _ = small_file(home/'.local/bin/claude-switch.sh')
000759 |             target = config_literals(data).get('TZ_TARGET')
000760 |     validate_timezone_target(target)
000761 |     return target

000764 | def validate_timezone_target(target):
000765 |     if (not isinstance(target, str) or not 0 < len(target) <= 80 or
000766 |             not re.fullmatch('[A-Za-z0-9/_+-]+', target) or
000767 |             any(part in ('', '.', '..') for part in target.split('/')) or
000768 |             not (ZONEINFO/target).is_file()):
000769 |         raise ValueError('configured timezone target is missing or invalid')

000772 | def watchdog_payload(target):
000773 |     validate_timezone_target(target)
000774 |     # Fixed reviewed equivalent of daemons/tz-watchdog.sh; only a validated
000775 |     # timezone literal is substituted. No user-controlled command or path.
000776 |     script = ('#!/bin/bash\nTARGET="' + target + '"\n'
000777 |               'current=$(readlink /etc/localtime 2>/dev/null)\n'
000778 |               'if [ "$current" != "/var/db/timezone/zoneinfo/$TARGET" ]; then\n'
000779 |               '    if /usr/sbin/systemsetup -settimezone "$TARGET"; then\n'
000780 |               '        logger -t local.tz-watchdog "时区异常: [$current] 已恢复为 $TARGET"\n'
000781 |               '    else\n'
000782 |               '        logger -t local.tz-watchdog "时区恢复失败: [$current] 目标为 $TARGET"\n'
000783 |               '        exit 1\n'
000784 |               '    fi\nfi\n').encode()
000785 |     plist = plistlib.dumps(dict(Label='local.tz-watchdog',
000786 |                                ProgramArguments=['/bin/bash', str(SYSTEM/'tz-watchdog.sh')],
000787 |                                StartInterval=3600, RunAtLoad=True))
000788 |     return [('script', SYSTEM/'tz-watchdog.sh', script, 0o755),
000789 |             ('plist', WATCHDOG_PLIST, plist, 0o644)]

000806 | def validate_proxy_mode(mode):
000807 |     if mode not in ('auto', 'clash-verge', 'shadowrocket', 'sing-box', 'clashx', 'surge'):
000808 |         raise ValueError('configured proxy mode is invalid')

000810 | 
000811 | # Reviewed fixed legacy template; never load root-executed code from a user path.
000812 | BODYGUARD_SCRIPT = '#!/bin/bash\n# Claude 保镖 (参考模板) —— 安装程序会把 __HOME__ / __PROXY_MODE__ 替换为实际值后安装\n# 作用: 代理(内核或TUN)离线时,自动关闭 Claude,杜绝裸连重试\nMODE="__PROXY_MODE__"\n\nok=1\n\n# TUN 在线判定:存在 fake-ip 网段地址即视为 TUN 已接管\nifconfig 2>/dev/null | grep -q \'inet 198\\.18\\.\' || ok=0\n\n# 内核在线判定:按安装时识别的客户端分派\ncase "$MODE" in\n    clash-verge)\n        pgrep -f verge-mihomo >/dev/null 2>&1 || ok=0 ;;\n    shadowrocket)\n        # launchd 环境下 scutil 可能查不到 VPN 行,查不到时以 TUN 判定为准(避免误杀)\n        nc=$(scutil --nc list 2>/dev/null)\n        if echo "$nc" | grep -q \'com.liguangming.Shadowrocket\'; then\n            echo "$nc" | grep \'com.liguangming.Shadowrocket\' | grep -q \'(Connected)\' || ok=0\n        fi ;;\n    sing-box)\n        pgrep -f sing-box >/dev/null 2>&1 || ok=0 ;;\n    clashx)\n        { pgrep -x ClashX >/dev/null 2>&1 || pgrep -f clash >/dev/null 2>&1; } || ok=0 ;;\n    surge)\n        pgrep -f Surge >/dev/null 2>&1 || ok=0 ;;\n    auto)\n        : ;;  # auto 模式只查 TUN\n    *)\n        pgrep -f verge-mihomo >/dev/null 2>&1 || ok=0 ;;\nesac\n\nif [ "$ok" -eq 0 ]; then\n    if pgrep -f \'/Applications/Claude.app/Contents/MacOS/Claude\' >/dev/null 2>&1; then\n        pkill -f \'/Applications/Claude.app/Contents/MacOS/Claude\'\n        logger -t local.claude-bodyguard "代理离线(内核或TUN丢失),已自动关闭 Claude"\n    fi\nfi\n'

000831 | def watchdog_backup(j, kind="watchdog"):
000832 |     target, payload, service_plist, label = root_service_spec(j, kind)
000833 |     if target is None:
000834 |         return
000835 |     previous = {}

000840 |             if metadata.st_uid != 0 or metadata.st_gid != 0 or stat.S_IMODE(metadata.st_mode) != mode:

000846 |             previous[key] = None

000853 | def root_service_ownership(files):
000854 |     """Pin and validate all fixed service files before correcting inherited GIDs."""
000855 |     with contextlib.ExitStack() as stack:
000856 |         opened = []
000857 |         for path, expected, mode in files:
000858 |             safe_parents(path.parent)
000859 |             parent = stack.enter_context(directory_fd(path.parent))
000860 |             fd = os.open(path.name, os.O_RDONLY | os.O_NOFOLLOW | os.O_NONBLOCK, dir_fd=parent)
000861 |             stream = stack.enter_context(os.fdopen(fd, 'rb'))
000862 |             metadata = os.fstat(fd)
000863 |             if (not stat.S_ISREG(metadata.st_mode) or metadata.st_nlink != 1 or
000864 |                     metadata.st_uid != 0 or stat.S_IMODE(metadata.st_mode) != mode or
000865 |                     metadata.st_size != len(expected) or stream.read() != expected):
000866 |                 raise ValueError('root service ownership repair refused: file differs')
000867 |             current = os.stat(path.name, dir_fd=parent, follow_symlinks=False)
000868 |             if (current.st_dev, current.st_ino) != (metadata.st_dev, metadata.st_ino):
000869 |                 raise ValueError('root service ownership repair refused: file replaced')
000870 |             opened.append((fd, metadata, parent, path.name))
000871 |         for fd, metadata, parent, name in opened:
000872 |             gid = metadata.st_gid
000873 |             if gid != 0:
000874 |                 os.fchown(fd, 0, 0)
000875 |                 os.fsync(fd)
000876 |             current = os.stat(name, dir_fd=parent, follow_symlinks=False)
000877 |             if (current.st_dev, current.st_ino) != (metadata.st_dev, metadata.st_ino):
000878 |                 raise ValueError('root service ownership repair refused: file replaced')

000934 |             raise ValueError(label + ' backup differs')

000945 |     output=run('/bin/launchctl','print-disabled',domain).stdout
000946 |     value=re.findall(r'"'+re.escape(label)+r'"\s*=>\s*(true|false)',output)

000952 | def legacy_snapshot(owner):
000953 |     result=[]
000954 |     for spec in legacy_specs(owner):
000955 |         path=Path(spec['path'])
000956 |         with as_owner(owner) if spec['uid'] else contextlib.nullcontext():
000957 |             try:
000958 |                 data,meta=small_file(path)
000959 |             except FileNotFoundError:
000960 |                 if run('/bin/launchctl','print',spec['domain']+'/'+spec['label'],check=False).returncode==0:
000961 |                     raise ValueError('legacy service registered without its fixed plist')
000962 |                 continue
000963 |         if meta.st_uid!=spec['uid'] or stat.S_IMODE(meta.st_mode)!=0o644:
000964 |             raise ValueError('legacy plist owner/mode differs: '+str(path))
000965 |         if spec['uid']:
000966 |             account=pwd.getpwuid(spec['uid'])
000967 |             if meta.st_gid not in os.getgrouplist(account.pw_name,account.pw_gid):
000968 |                 raise ValueError('legacy plist group cannot be restored by its owner: '+str(path))
000969 |         expected=dict(Label=spec['label'],ProgramArguments=spec['arguments'],
000970 |                       RunAtLoad=spec['run'],StartInterval=spec['interval'])
000971 |         if plistlib.loads(data)!=expected:
000972 |             raise ValueError('legacy plist is not the fixed supported template: '+str(path))
000973 |         if not spec['uid']:
000974 |             safe_parents(path.parent)
000975 |             script=Path(spec['arguments'][1]); safe_parents(script.parent)
000976 |             script_data,script_meta=small_file(script)
000977 |             if script_meta.st_uid or script_meta.st_mode & 0o022:
000978 |                 raise ValueError('unsafe legacy root script')
000979 |             script_hash=hashlib.sha256(script_data).hexdigest()
000980 |         else:
000981 |             script_hash=None
000982 |         result.append(dict(spec,sha256=hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest(),gid=meta.st_gid,mode=0o644,
000983 |                            script_sha256=script_hash,
000984 |                            registered=run('/bin/launchctl','print',spec['domain']+'/'+spec['label'],check=False).returncode==0,
000985 |                            disabled=legacy_disabled(spec['domain'],spec['label'])))
000986 |     return result

001021 | def legacy_restore(j):
001022 |     for item in j['plan']['legacy']:
001023 |         j['pending']='restore-legacy-'+item['label']; save(JOURNAL,j)
001024 |         data,_=small_file(Path(j['stage'])/('legacy-'+item['label']+'.plist'))
001025 |         if hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()!=item['sha256']:
001026 |             raise ValueError('legacy backup differs')
001027 |         if not item['uid']:
001028 |             script_data,meta=small_file(Path(item['arguments'][1]))
001029 |             if meta.st_uid or meta.st_mode & 0o022 or hashlib.sha256(script_data).hexdigest()!=item['script_sha256']:
001030 |                 raise ValueError('legacy root script changed; cannot reactivate')
001031 |         path=Path(item['path'])
001032 |         with as_owner(item['uid']) if item['uid'] else contextlib.nullcontext():
001033 |             with directory_fd(path.parent) as parent:
001034 |                 try:
001035 |                     present,_=small_file(path)
001036 |                 except FileNotFoundError:
001037 |                     fd=os.open(path.name,os.O_WRONLY|os.O_CREAT|os.O_EXCL|os.O_NOFOLLOW,0o644,dir_fd=parent)
001038 |                     with os.fdopen(fd,'wb') as stream:
001039 |                         os.fchown(stream.fileno(),item['uid'],item['gid'])
001040 |                         stream.write(data); stream.flush(); os.fchmod(stream.fileno(),item['mode']); os.fsync(stream.fileno())
001041 |                     os.fsync(parent)
001042 |                 else:
001043 |                     if present!=data:
001044 |                         raise ValueError('legacy destination changed; refusing overwrite')
001045 |                     fd=os.open(path.name,os.O_RDONLY|os.O_NOFOLLOW|os.O_NONBLOCK,dir_fd=parent)
001046 |                     try:
001047 |                         metadata=os.fstat(fd)
001048 |                         if not stat.S_ISREG(metadata.st_mode) or metadata.st_uid!=item['uid'] or metadata.st_nlink!=1:
001049 |                             raise ValueError('legacy destination metadata changed')
001050 |                         os.fchown(fd,item['uid'],item['gid'])
001051 |                         os.fchmod(fd,item['mode']); os.fsync(fd)
001052 |                     finally:
001053 |                         os.close(fd)
001054 |         if legacy_disabled(item['domain'],item['label'])!=item['disabled']:
001055 |             raise ValueError('legacy disabled override changed; refusing to modify it')
001056 |         target=item['domain']+'/'+item['label']
001057 |         registered=run('/bin/launchctl','print',target,check=False).returncode==0
001058 |         if item['registered'] and not registered:
001059 |             # Restoring RunAtLoad may immediately run the original scheduled task.
001060 |             run('/bin/launchctl','bootstrap',item['domain'],path)
001061 |         elif registered and not item['registered']:
001062 |             raise ValueError('unexpected legacy service registration')
001063 |     if legacy_snapshot(j['plan']['owner_uid'])!=j['plan']['legacy']:
001064 |         raise ValueError('legacy restoration differs from preview')

001066 | 
001067 | @contextlib.contextmanager
001068 | def lifecycle_lock(owner):
001069 |     home = Path(pwd.getpwuid(owner).pw_dir)
001070 |     directory = home/'.claude-switcher/v2'
001071 |     # Root never creates or traverses a user-owned directory. Drop effective UID
001072 |     # while opening the same lifecycle flock used by the GUI, then retain its FD.
001073 |     os.seteuid(owner)
001074 |     try:
001075 |         for parent in [home, home/'.claude-switcher', directory]:
001076 |             if parent.is_symlink():
001077 |                 raise ValueError('unsafe lifecycle directory')
001078 |             if not parent.exists():
001079 |                 parent.mkdir(mode=0o700)
001080 |             m = parent.stat()
001081 |             if m.st_uid != owner or m.st_mode & 0o022:
001082 |                 raise ValueError('unsafe lifecycle owner/mode')
001083 |         fd = os.open(directory/'lifecycle.lock', os.O_RDWR | os.O_CREAT | os.O_NOFOLLOW, 0o600)
001084 |     finally:
001085 |         os.seteuid(0)
001086 |     try:
001087 |         m = os.fstat(fd)
001088 |         if not stat.S_ISREG(m.st_mode) or m.st_uid != owner or m.st_nlink != 1 or m.st_mode & 0o077:
001089 |             raise ValueError('unsafe lifecycle lock')
001090 |         fcntl.flock(fd, fcntl.LOCK_EX | fcntl.LOCK_NB)
001091 |         yield
001092 |     finally:
001093 |         os.close(fd)
001094 | 
001095 | 
001096 | @contextlib.contextmanager
001097 | def locks(owner, previous_owner=None):
001098 |     with contextlib.ExitStack() as stack:
001099 |         for uid in sorted({owner, previous_owner} - {None}):
001100 |             stack.enter_context(lifecycle_lock(uid))
001101 |         safe_parents(SYSTEM.parent)
001102 |         SYSTEM.mkdir(mode=0o755, exist_ok=True)
001103 |         safe_parents(SYSTEM)
001104 |         rootfd = os.open(SYSTEM/'root-operation.lock', os.O_RDWR | os.O_CREAT | os.O_NOFOLLOW, 0o600)
001105 |         try:
001106 |             metadata = os.fstat(rootfd)
001107 |             if not stat.S_ISREG(metadata.st_mode) or metadata.st_uid or metadata.st_nlink != 1 or metadata.st_mode & 0o022:
001108 |                 raise ValueError('unsafe root lock')
001109 |             fcntl.flock(rootfd, fcntl.LOCK_EX | fcntl.LOCK_NB)
001110 |             yield
001111 |         finally:
001112 |             os.close(rootfd)

001115 | def service(action, owner, check=True, only=None):
001116 |     for domain, label, plist in [('gui/'+str(owner), 'local.claudeswitcher.guardian', AGENT),
001117 |                                   ('system', 'local.claudeswitcher.helper', DAEMON)]:
001118 |         target = domain+'/'+label
001119 |         if only is not None and target not in only:
001120 |             continue
001121 |         if action == 'bootout':
001122 |             if run('/bin/launchctl', 'print', target, check=False).returncode == 0:
001123 |                 run('/bin/launchctl', 'bootout', target, check=check)
001124 |         elif plist.exists():
001125 |             run('/bin/launchctl', 'bootstrap', domain, plist, check=check)
001126 |             if domain == 'system':
001127 |                 run('/bin/launchctl', 'kickstart', target, check=check)

001146 | def load_journal():
001147 |     m = JOURNAL.lstat()
001148 |     if not stat.S_ISREG(m.st_mode) or m.st_uid or m.st_mode & 0o077 or m.st_nlink != 1:
001149 |         raise ValueError('unsafe journal')
001150 |     return json.loads(JOURNAL.read_text())

001152 | 
001153 | def present(path):
001154 |     # lstat also detects dangling links; pending repair state must never disappear.
001155 |     try:
001156 |         path.lstat()
001157 |         return True
001158 |     except FileNotFoundError:
001159 |         return False
001160 | 
001161 | 
001162 | def managed_json(path, mode):
001163 |     safe_parents(path.parent)
001164 |     def unique(pairs):
001165 |         result = {}
001166 |         for key, value in pairs:
001167 |             if key in result:
001168 |                 raise ValueError('duplicate managed state JSON key')
001169 |             result[key] = value
001170 |         return result
001171 |     fd = os.open(path, os.O_RDONLY | os.O_NOFOLLOW | os.O_NONBLOCK)
001172 |     with os.fdopen(fd, 'rb') as stream:
001173 |         before = os.fstat(stream.fileno())
001174 |         if (not stat.S_ISREG(before.st_mode) or before.st_uid != 0 or before.st_nlink != 1 or
001175 |                 stat.S_IMODE(before.st_mode) != mode or before.st_size > 16384):
001176 |             raise ValueError('unsafe managed state metadata')
001177 |         data = stream.read(16385)
001178 |         after = os.fstat(stream.fileno())
001179 |         fields = ('st_dev','st_ino','st_uid','st_gid','st_mode','st_nlink','st_size','st_mtime_ns','st_ctime_ns')
001180 |         snapshot = {key:getattr(before,key) for key in fields}
001181 |         if len(data) > 16384 or snapshot != {key:getattr(after,key) for key in fields}:
001182 |             raise ValueError('managed state changed while reading')
001183 |         snapshot['sha256'] = hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
001184 |         return json.loads(data, object_pairs_hook=unique), snapshot
001185 | 
001186 | 
001187 | def managed_results_directory():
001188 |     path = SYSTEM/'repair-results'
001189 |     safe_parents(path.parent)
001190 |     fd = os.open(path, os.O_RDONLY | os.O_DIRECTORY | os.O_NOFOLLOW | os.O_NONBLOCK)
001191 |     try:
001192 |         metadata = os.fstat(fd)
001193 |         if not stat.S_ISDIR(metadata.st_mode) or metadata.st_uid != 0 or metadata.st_mode & 0o077:
001194 |             raise ValueError('unsafe managed repair directory')
001195 |         current = path.lstat()
001196 |         if (current.st_dev, current.st_ino) != (metadata.st_dev, metadata.st_ino):
001197 |             raise ValueError('managed repair directory identity changed')
001198 |         # Receipt creation can change directory timestamps; identity and access
001199 |         # permissions, rather than timestamps, define the directory snapshot.
001200 |         return {key:getattr(metadata,key) for key in ('st_dev','st_ino','st_uid','st_gid','st_mode')}
001201 |     finally:
001202 |         os.close(fd)

001205 | def recheck_managed(j):
001206 |     if 'managed_system' in j:
001207 |         # Components may already be replaced. Validate the immutable repair files
001208 |         # against the snapshot without treating candidate code as the old record.
001209 |         for name in ('repair-journal.json', '.repair-journal.next', '.system-original.next'):
001210 |             if present(SYSTEM/name):
001211 |                 raise ValueError('managed state conflict; transaction retained')
001212 |         expected = j['managed_system']
001213 |         if managed_results_directory() != expected['results_directory']:
001214 |             raise ValueError('managed repair directory conflict; transaction retained')
001215 |         for key, path in [('original', SYSTEM/'system-original.json'),
001216 |                           ('receipt', SYSTEM/'repair-results'/(expected['plan_id']+'.json'))]:
001217 |             _, observed = managed_json(path, 0o600)
001218 |             if observed != expected[key]:
001219 |                 raise ValueError('managed state conflict; transaction retained')
001220 |         if managed_results_directory() != expected['results_directory']:
001221 |             raise ValueError('managed repair directory conflict; transaction retained')

001223 | 
001224 | def transact(p, expected):
001225 |     if digest(p) != expected:
001226 |         raise ValueError('plan digest changed; preview again')
001227 |     if present(JOURNAL) or present(BARRIER):
001228 |         raise ValueError('recovery required; an installation is pending')
001229 |     # Repair records belong to the helper. Preserve them without making their
001230 |     # semantic state an installer prerequisite. Old journals retain rechecks.
001231 |     managed = None
001232 |     for target in p['destinations']:
001233 |         safe_parents(Path(target).parent)
001234 |         if Path(target).is_symlink():
001235 |             raise ValueError('destination symlink')
001236 |     if RECORD.exists():
001237 |         old = private_json(RECORD,0o644)
001238 |         if old['owner_uid'] != p.get('previous_owner_uid',p['owner_uid']):
001239 |             raise ValueError('previous owner changed since preview')
001240 |         if old['edition'] != p.get('previous_edition',p['edition']):
001241 |             raise ValueError('previous edition changed since preview')
001242 |         if p.get('previous_edition') and old['trust']['gui_cdhash'] != p['previous']['old_app']['cdhash']:
001243 |             raise ValueError('previous edition identity changed since preview')
001244 |     if plan_state(p) != p['previous']:
001245 |         raise ValueError('installed components changed since preview')
001246 |     ensure_system_directories()
001247 |     stage = SYSTEM / ('source-local-' + uuid.uuid4().hex)
001248 |     stage.mkdir(mode=0o700)
001249 |     generation = stage.name.removeprefix('source-local-')
001250 |     for key, item in p['payload'].items():
001251 |         target = stage/key

001253 |         identifier = 'local.claudeswitcher.' + (p['edition'] if key == 'app' else key)
001254 |         if tree(target) != item['sha256'] or identity(target, identifier) != item['cdhash']:
001255 |             raise ValueError('staged payload differs from approved plan')

001257 |         for key, target in [('daemon', DAEMON), ('agent', AGENT)]:
001258 |             copy(TEMPLATES/target.name, stage/key)
001259 |             if hashlib.sha256((stage/key).read_bytes()).hexdigest() != p['templates'][target.name]:
001260 |                 raise ValueError('template changed')

001262 |              trust=dict(kind='source_local', generation=generation,
001263 |                         **{('gui' if k == 'app' else k)+'_cdhash': v['cdhash'] for k,v in p['payload'].items()}),
001264 |              helper_version=p['version'], minimum_app_version=p['version']))
001265 |         (stage/'record').chmod(0o644)

001267 |         raise ValueError('installed components changed since preview')
001268 |     keys = component_keys(p)
001269 |     active = transaction_registered(p)
001270 |     j = dict(plan=p, stage=str(stage), pending='backup', completed=[], old=[], status='pending', active=active)
001271 |     if managed is not None:
001272 |         j['managed_system'] = managed
001273 |     save(JOURNAL, j)
001274 |     for key, target in zip(keys, p['destinations']):
001275 |         if Path(target).exists():
001276 |             backup=stage/('old-'+key)
001277 |             copy(Path(target), backup)
001278 |             expected_old=p['previous'][key]
001279 |             if expected_old is None or tree(backup)!=expected_old['sha256']:
001280 |                 raise ValueError('backup differs from approved previous component')
001281 |             if 'cdhash' in expected_old:
001282 |                 role=p['previous_edition'] if key=='old_app' else (p['edition'] if key=='app' else key)
001283 |                 if identity(backup,'local.claudeswitcher.'+role)!=expected_old['cdhash']:
001284 |                     raise ValueError('backup code identity differs')
001285 |             j['old'].append(key)
001286 |             save(JOURNAL, j)
001287 |     legacy_backup(j)
001288 |     cli_backup(j)
001289 |     watchdog_backup(j)
001290 |     watchdog_backup(j, "bodyguard")
001291 |     if plan_state(p)!=p['previous']:
001292 |         raise ValueError('installed components changed during backup')
001293 |     j['pending'] = 'services'; save(JOURNAL, j)
001294 |     boot = boot_uuid()
001295 |     recheck_managed(j)
001296 |     fd = os.open(BARRIER, os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_EXCL | os.O_NOFOLLOW, 0o644)
001297 |     with os.fdopen(fd, 'w') as barrier:
001298 |         barrier.write(boot)
001299 |         barrier.flush()
001300 |         os.fsync(barrier.fileno())
001301 |     syncdir(SYSTEM)
001302 |     recheck_managed(j)
001303 |     for uid in transaction_owners(p):
001304 |         stopped(uid)
001305 |     legacy_migrate(j)
001306 |     transaction_service('bootout', p)
001307 |     recheck_managed(j)
001308 |     pairs=list(zip(keys,p['destinations']))
001309 |     # Retire the old edition before publishing the new one; rollback removes
001310 |     # the new edition before restoring the old App.
001311 |     pairs.sort(key=lambda item:item[0] != 'old_app')
001312 |     for key, target in pairs:
001313 |         j['pending'] = key; save(JOURNAL, j)
001314 |         replace_component(stage/key if p['operation'] != 'uninstall' and key != 'old_app' else None,
001315 |                           Path(target), stage, is_app=key in ('app','old_app'))
001316 |         j['completed'].append(key); save(JOURNAL, j)
001317 |     cli_install(j)
001318 |     watchdog_install(j)
001319 |     watchdog_install(j, "bodyguard")
001320 |     j['pending'] = 'health'; save(JOURNAL, j)
001321 |     if p['operation'] != 'uninstall':
001322 |         service('bootstrap', p['owner_uid'])
001323 |     return dict(status='pending_component_verification', recovery='sudo python3 packaging/macos/local_install.py recover')
001324 | 
001325 | 
001326 | def recover(owner):
001327 |     terminal = resume_terminal(owner)
001328 |     if terminal is not None:
001329 |         return terminal
001330 |     j = load_journal()
001331 |     if j['plan']['owner_uid'] != owner:
001332 |         raise ValueError('console owner differs from journal')
001333 |     recheck_managed(j)
001334 |     stage = Path(j['stage'])
001335 |     if stage.parent != SYSTEM or not re.fullmatch('source-local-[a-f0-9]{32}', stage.name):
001336 |         raise ValueError('unsafe recovery stage')
001337 |     safe_parents(stage)
001338 |     # Backup never mutated destinations. Preserve this terminal fact across retries.
001339 |     if j['pending'] in ('backup', 'backup_aborted'):
001340 |         if plan_state(j['plan']) != j['plan']['previous'] or legacy_snapshot(owner)!=j['plan']['legacy']:
001341 |             raise ValueError('untouched installation changed; manual recovery required')
001342 |         return finish(j,'backup_aborted',dict(installed_unchanged=True))
001343 |     if j['pending'] != 'restored_health':
001344 |         transaction_service('bootout', j['plan'])
001345 |         for key, target in zip(component_keys(j['plan']), j['plan']['destinations']):
001346 |             allowed=[app(j['plan']['edition']),HELPER,GUARDIAN,DAEMON,AGENT,RECORD]
001347 |             if j['plan'].get('previous_edition') in ('personal','public'):
001348 |                 allowed.append(app(j['plan']['previous_edition']))
001349 |             if target not in list(map(str,allowed)):
001350 |                 raise ValueError('unsafe recovery target')
001351 |             safe_parents(Path(target).parent)
001352 |             j['pending'] = 'restore-'+key; save(JOURNAL,j)
001353 |             replace_component(stage/('old-'+key) if key in j['old'] else None,
001354 |                               Path(target), stage, is_app=key in ('app','old_app'))
001355 |         cli_restore(j)
001356 |         watchdog_restore(j)
001357 |         watchdog_restore(j, "bodyguard")
001358 |         legacy_restore(j)
001359 |         transaction_service('bootstrap', j['plan'], only=j['active'])
001360 |     j['pending'] = 'restored_health'; save(JOURNAL,j)
001361 |     observed = plan_state(j['plan'])
001362 |     # Files copied by root remain root-owned; byte/mode/signature restoration is exact.
001363 |     for key, previous in j['plan']['previous'].items():
001364 |         current = observed[key]
001365 |         if previous is None:
001366 |             if current is not None:
001367 |                 raise ValueError('recovery left an unexpected component')
001368 |         elif current is None or any(current.get(field) != previous.get(field) for field in ['sha256','mode','cdhash']):
001369 |             raise ValueError('restored component differs: '+key)
001370 |     registered = transaction_registered(j['plan'])
001371 |     if sorted(registered) != sorted(j['active']):
001372 |         raise ValueError('restored service registration differs')
001373 |     return finish(j,'restored',dict(components=observed, registered=registered))
001374 | 
001375 | 
001376 | def registered_services(owner):
001377 |     return [target for target in ['system/local.claudeswitcher.helper',
001378 |             'gui/'+str(owner)+'/local.claudeswitcher.guardian']
001379 |             if run('/bin/launchctl','print',target,check=False).returncode == 0]
001380 | 
001381 | 
001382 | def verify_components(j):
001383 |     if j['plan'].get('previous_edition') and present(app(j['plan']['previous_edition'])):
001384 |         raise ValueError('previous edition App remains installed')
001385 |     record = private_json(RECORD,0o644)
001386 |     expected_record = private_json(Path(j['stage'])/'record',0o644)
001387 |     if record != expected_record:
001388 |         raise ValueError('installed record differs from staged record')
001389 |     for key, path in [('app',app(record['edition'])), ('helper',HELPER), ('guardian',GUARDIAN)]:
001390 |         expected = j['plan']['payload'][key]
001391 |         role = record['edition'] if key == 'app' else key
001392 |         if tree(path) != expected['sha256'] or identity(path,'local.claudeswitcher.'+role) != expected['cdhash']:
001393 |             raise ValueError('installed identity differs: '+key)
001394 |     for key,path in [('daemon',DAEMON),('agent',AGENT)]:
001395 |         if tree(path) != tree(Path(j['stage'])/key):
001396 |             raise ValueError('installed service template differs')
001397 |     return record
001398 | 
001399 | 
001400 | def verify_processes(owner):
001401 |     processes = []
001402 |     for domain, label, path, uid in [('system','local.claudeswitcher.helper',HELPER,0),
001403 |             ('gui/'+str(owner),'local.claudeswitcher.guardian',GUARDIAN,owner)]:
001404 |         state = run('/bin/launchctl','print',domain+'/'+label).stdout
001405 |         pid = re.findall(r'^\s*pid = (\d+)\s*$',state,re.M)
001406 |         program = re.findall(r'^\s*program = (.+)$',state,re.M)
001407 |         if len(pid)!=1 or program != [str(path)]:
001408 |             raise ValueError('registered live service identity unavailable: '+label)
001409 |         process = run('/bin/ps','-ww','-p',pid[0],'-o','uid=,comm=').stdout.strip().split(None,1)
001410 |         if len(process)!=2 or int(process[0])!=uid or process[1]!=str(path):
001411 |             raise ValueError('live process differs: '+label)
001412 |         processes.append(dict(label=label,pid=int(pid[0]),uid=uid,path=str(path)))
001413 |     return processes
001414 | 
001415 | 
001416 | def create_challenge(j):
001417 |     record = verify_components(j)
001418 |     challenge = dict(schema_version=1,record=record,generation=record['trust']['generation'],
001419 |                      nonce=secrets.token_hex(32),boot_session_uuid=boot_uuid(),expires_at=int(time.time())+110)
001420 |     if RECEIPT.exists():
001421 |         RECEIPT.unlink(); syncdir(SYSTEM)
001422 |     save(CHALLENGE,challenge,mode=0o644)
001423 |     return challenge
001424 | 
001425 | 
001426 | def valid_receipt(challenge, record):
001427 |     now=int(time.time())
001428 |     if (challenge.get('schema_version') != 1 or challenge.get('record') != record or
001429 |             challenge.get('generation') != record['trust']['generation'] or
001430 |             not re.fullmatch('[a-f0-9]{64}',challenge.get('nonce','')) or
001431 |             challenge.get('boot_session_uuid') != boot_uuid() or
001432 |             type(challenge.get('expires_at')) is not int or not now <= challenge['expires_at'] <= now+120):
001433 |         raise ValueError('expired or mismatched health challenge')
001434 |     receipt=private_json(RECEIPT,0o600)
001435 |     if (set(receipt) != {'schema_version','challenge','gui_verified','guardian_verified','guardian_loop_sequence','observed_at'} or
001436 |             receipt['schema_version'] != 1 or receipt['challenge'] != challenge or
001437 |             receipt['gui_verified'] is not True or receipt['guardian_verified'] is not True or
001438 |             type(receipt['guardian_loop_sequence']) is not int or receipt['guardian_loop_sequence'] <= 0 or
001439 |             type(receipt['observed_at']) is not int or
001440 |             not challenge['expires_at']-120 <= receipt['observed_at'] <= min(now,challenge['expires_at'])):
001441 |         raise ValueError('authenticated health receipt rejected')
001442 |     return receipt
001443 | 
001444 | 
001445 | def launch_health(j, challenge):
001446 |     owner=j['plan']['owner_uid']; account=pwd.getpwuid(owner)
001447 |     bundle=app(j['plan']['edition'])
001448 |     meta=plistlib.loads((bundle/'Contents/Info.plist').read_bytes())
001449 |     name=meta['CFBundleExecutable']
001450 |     if not isinstance(name,str) or '/' in name or name in ('','.','..'):
001451 |         raise ValueError('invalid GUI executable')
001452 |     executable=bundle/'Contents/MacOS'/name
001453 |     # launchctl establishes the owner's GUI namespace; sudo then drops uid/gid.
001454 |     command=['/bin/launchctl','asuser',str(owner),'/usr/bin/sudo','-n','-u','#'+str(owner),
001455 |              '-g','#'+str(account.pw_gid),'--','/usr/bin/env','-i','HOME='+account.pw_dir,
001456 |              'PATH=/usr/bin:/bin:/usr/sbin:/sbin',str(executable),'--installation-health',
001457 |              challenge['generation'],challenge['nonce']]
001458 |     return subprocess.Popen(command,stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL,
001459 |                             stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL,env={'PATH':'/usr/bin:/bin:/usr/sbin:/sbin'})
001460 | 
001461 | 
001462 | def finalize(owner, launch=False, authenticated=False):
001463 |     terminal=resume_terminal(owner)
001464 |     if terminal is not None:
001465 |         return terminal
001466 |     j=load_journal()
001467 |     if j['plan']['owner_uid'] != owner or j['pending'] != 'health':
001468 |         raise ValueError('transaction requires recovery')
001469 |     if j['plan']['operation']=='uninstall':
001470 |         if any(Path(p).exists() or Path(p).is_symlink() for p in j['plan']['destinations']) or registered_services(owner):
001471 |             raise ValueError('uninstall is incomplete')
001472 |         return finish(j,'uninstalled',dict(components_absent=True,services_absent=True))
001473 |     recheck_managed(j)
001474 |     record=verify_components(j)
001475 |     previous_owner=j['plan'].get('previous_owner_uid')
001476 |     if previous_owner is not None and 'gui/'+str(previous_owner)+'/local.claudeswitcher.guardian' in registered_services(previous_owner):
001477 |         raise ValueError('previous owner guardian is still registered')
001478 |     pending_service_ownership(j)
001479 |     watchdog_verify(j)
001480 |     watchdog_verify(j, "bodyguard")
001481 |     cli_verify(j)
001482 |     if not authenticated:
001483 |         processes=verify_processes(owner)
001484 |         return finish(j,'committed',dict(components_verified=True, processes=processes,
001485 |                                         authenticated_health='not_run'))
001486 |     if launch:
001487 |         challenge=create_challenge(j)
001488 |         # Retry after reboot re-registers only missing services, never uses old sessions.
001489 |         for domain,label,plist in [('system','local.claudeswitcher.helper',DAEMON),
001490 |                                   ('gui/'+str(owner),'local.claudeswitcher.guardian',AGENT)]:
001491 |             if run('/bin/launchctl','print',domain+'/'+label,check=False).returncode != 0:
001492 |                 run('/bin/launchctl','bootstrap',domain,plist)
001493 |         child=launch_health(j,challenge)
001494 |         deadline=time.monotonic()+110
001495 |         while time.monotonic() < deadline:
001496 |             if RECEIPT.exists():
001497 |                 break
001498 |             if child.poll() not in (None,0):
001499 |                 raise ValueError('installation-health GUI failed; use recover or finalize')
001500 |             time.sleep(0.25)
001501 |         # Do not kill other GUI processes. This particular health probe has its own
001502 |         # deadline; terminate only our child if it overstays after the bounded wait.
001503 |         if child.poll() is None:
001504 |             try:
001505 |                 child.wait(timeout=max(0.1,min(2,deadline-time.monotonic())))
001506 |             except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
001507 |                 child.terminate()
001508 |         if not RECEIPT.exists():
001509 |             raise ValueError('health timed out; transaction retained for finalize or recover')
001510 |     challenge=private_json(CHALLENGE,0o644)
001511 |     receipt=valid_receipt(challenge,record)
001512 |     processes=verify_processes(owner)
001513 |     verify_components(j)
001514 |     return finish(j,'committed',dict(receipt=receipt,processes=processes))
001515 | 
001516 | 
001517 | def verify(owner):
001518 |     if not JOURNAL.exists():
001519 |         result=private_json(LAST_RESULT,0o600)
001520 |         if result['owner_uid'] != owner:
001521 |             raise ValueError('console owner differs from completed transaction')
001522 |         stage=Path(result['stage'])
001523 |         if stage.parent!=SYSTEM or not re.fullmatch('source-local-[a-f0-9]{32}',stage.name):
001524 |             raise ValueError('unsafe completed transaction stage')
001525 |         archived=private_json(stage/'result.json',0o600)
001526 |         if result['status']=='committed':
001527 |             verify_components(archived)
001528 |             result['processes']=verify_processes(owner)
001529 |         elif result['status']=='uninstalled':
001530 |             if any(Path(p).exists() or Path(p).is_symlink() for p in archived['plan']['destinations']) or registered_services(owner):
001531 |                 raise ValueError('uninstalled components reappeared')
001532 |         result['evidence']=str(stage/'result.json')
001533 |         return result
001534 |     j=load_journal()
001535 |     if j['plan']['owner_uid'] != owner:
001536 |         raise ValueError('console owner differs from journal')
001537 |     if j['pending']=='health' and j['plan']['operation']!='uninstall':
001538 |         verify_components(j)
001539 |         return dict(status='pending_authenticated_health',processes=verify_processes(owner))
001540 |     return dict(status='pending_recovery',phase=j['pending'])
001541 | 
001542 | 
001543 | def verify_public(owner, edition):
001544 |     record = private_json(RECORD, 0o644)
001545 |     if record['owner_uid'] != owner or record['edition'] != edition:
001546 |         raise ValueError('installation owner/edition differs')
001547 |     observed = installed_state(edition)
001548 |     if any(value is None for value in observed.values()):
001549 |         raise ValueError('installed component missing')
001550 |     if any(value['uid'] != 0 or value['mode'] & 0o022 for value in observed.values()):
001551 |         raise ValueError('installed component permissions differ')
001552 |     for key, pin in [('app','gui_cdhash'),('helper','helper_cdhash'),('guardian','guardian_cdhash')]:
001553 |         if observed[key]['cdhash'] != record['trust'][pin]:
001554 |             raise ValueError('installed component identity differs')
001555 |     watchdog_verify(dict(watchdog={}, plan=dict(timezone_target=read_timezone_target(owner))))
001556 |     watchdog_verify(dict(bodyguard={}, plan=dict(proxy_mode=read_proxy_mode(owner))), 'bodyguard')
001557 |     cli_verify(dict(plan=dict(owner_uid=owner,edition=edition,cli_entry=cli_snapshot(owner))))
001558 |     return dict(status='components_verified', processes=verify_processes(owner),
001559 |                 timezone_watchdog='registered', authenticated_health='not_run')
001560 | 
001561 | 
001562 | def main():
001563 |     parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
001564 |     parser.add_argument('operation', choices=['plan','install','replace','uninstall','recover','verify','finalize','commit'])
001565 |     parser.add_argument('--edition', choices=['personal','public'], default='personal')
001566 |     parser.add_argument('--app', type=Path)
001567 |     parser.add_argument('--digest')
001568 |     parser.add_argument('--uninstall-plan', action='store_true')
001569 |     args = parser.parse_args()
001570 |     owner = console_owner()
001571 |     if args.operation == 'plan':
001572 |         p = plan(args.edition, args.app, owner, 'uninstall' if args.uninstall_plan else 'install')
001573 |         print(json.dumps(dict(plan=p, digest=digest(p),
001574 |             notes=['Legacy rollback restores scheduling; RunAtLoad may immediately start its original task.'],
001575 |             legacy_running_observations={item['label']:bool(re.search(r'^\s*pid = \d+',
001576 |                 run('/bin/launchctl','print',item['domain']+'/'+item['label'],check=False).stdout,re.M))
001577 |                 for item in p['legacy']}), indent=2)); return
001578 |     if args.operation == 'verify':
001579 |         print(json.dumps(verify_public(owner, args.edition), indent=2)); return
001580 |     if os.geteuid() != 0:
001581 |         raise ValueError('explicit administrator invocation required')
001582 |     p = None
001583 |     if args.operation in ('install','replace','uninstall'):
001584 |         # First-run discovery must fail before creating locks or stopping apps.
001585 |         p = plan(args.edition, args.app, owner, 'uninstall' if args.operation == 'uninstall' else 'install')
001586 |         if args.digest is not None and args.digest != digest(p):
001587 |             raise ValueError('plan digest changed')
001588 |     previous_owner = p.get('previous_owner_uid') if p else None
001589 |     if p is None and JOURNAL.exists():
001590 |         previous_owner = load_journal()['plan'].get('previous_owner_uid')
001591 |     if args.operation in ('install','replace','uninstall','recover'):
001592 |         for uid in sorted({owner, previous_owner} - {None}):
001593 |             stopped(uid)
001594 |     with locks(owner, previous_owner):
001595 |         if args.operation == 'recover':
001596 |             result = recover(owner)
001597 |         elif args.operation in ('finalize','commit'):
001598 |             result = finalize(owner,launch=args.operation=='finalize')
001599 |         else:
001600 |             if args.operation == 'replace':
001601 |                 # Preview is identical; authorization to replace is the explicit command.
001602 |                 expected = digest(p)
001603 |                 if args.digest is not None and args.digest != expected:
001604 |                     raise ValueError('plan digest changed')
001605 |                 p['operation'] = 'replace'
001606 |                 result = transact(p, digest(p))
001607 |             else:
001608 |                 result = transact(p, args.digest or digest(p))
001609 |             result = finalize(owner,launch=p['operation']!='uninstall')
001610 |         print(json.dumps(result, indent=2))
001611 | 
001612 | 
001613 | if __name__ == '__main__':
001614 |     try:
001615 |         main()
001616 |     except (ValueError, OSError, subprocess.SubprocessError) as error:
001617 |         raise SystemExit(str(error))

001153 | class OwnerCredentialsTests(unittest.TestCase):
001154 |     def credentials(self, fail=None):
001155 |         stack = __import__('contextlib').ExitStack()
001156 |         self.addCleanup(stack.close)
001157 |         state = dict(uid=0, gid=0, groups=[0, 12])
001158 |         calls = []
001159 |         def change(name, value):
001160 |             calls.append((name, value))
001161 |             if name == fail and not any(c[0] == 'failed' for c in calls):
001162 |                 calls.append(('failed', name))
001163 |                 raise OSError('injected ' + name)
001164 |             state[name] = value
001165 |         stack.enter_context(patch.object(m.os, 'geteuid', side_effect=lambda: state['uid']))
001166 |         stack.enter_context(patch.object(m.os, 'getegid', side_effect=lambda: state['gid']))
001167 |         # This is the oversized directory membership view; production must not use it.
001168 |         directory_groups = stack.enter_context(patch.object(m.os, 'getgroups', return_value=list(range(64))))
001169 |         stack.enter_context(patch.object(m, 'process_groups', return_value=[0, 12]))
001170 |         stack.enter_context(patch.object(m.pwd, 'getpwuid', return_value=type('Account', (), dict(pw_name='owner', pw_gid=20))()))
001171 |         stack.enter_context(patch.object(m.os, 'initgroups', side_effect=lambda name, gid: change('groups', [20, 12])))
001172 |         stack.enter_context(patch.object(m.os, 'setgroups', side_effect=lambda groups: change('groups', groups)))
001173 |         stack.enter_context(patch.object(m.os, 'setegid', side_effect=lambda gid: change('gid', gid)))
001174 |         stack.enter_context(patch.object(m.os, 'seteuid', side_effect=lambda uid: change('uid', uid)))
001175 |         return state, calls, directory_groups
001176 | 
001177 |     def test_owner_restores_kernel_groups_not_oversized_directory_groups(self):
001178 |         state, calls, directory_groups = self.credentials()
001179 |         with m.as_owner(501):
001180 |             self.assertEqual(state, dict(uid=501, gid=20, groups=[20, 12]))
001181 |         self.assertEqual(state, dict(uid=0, gid=0, groups=[0, 12]))
001182 |         directory_groups.assert_not_called()
001183 | 
001184 |     def test_owner_restores_after_body_exception(self):
001185 |         state, calls, _ = self.credentials()
001186 |         with self.assertRaisesRegex(RuntimeError, 'body'):
001187 |             with m.as_owner(501):
001188 |                 raise RuntimeError('body')
001189 |         self.assertEqual(state, dict(uid=0, gid=0, groups=[0, 12]))
001190 |

000020 | def index_entry(index, name, version):

000034 | def collect(directory, explicit=()):
000035 |     """Only package license/notice/copyright files; never follow symlinks."""
000036 |     if not directory.is_dir() or directory.is_symlink():
000037 |         return []
000038 |     candidates = set()
000039 |     for item in directory.rglob("*"):
000040 |         relative = item.relative_to(directory)
000041 |         if any(p in ("node_modules", ".git", "target") for p in relative.parts):
000042 |             continue
000043 |         if item.is_symlink() or not item.is_file():
000044 |             continue
000045 |         if any((directory / Path(*relative.parts[:i])).is_symlink()
000046 |                for i in range(1, len(relative.parts))):
000047 |             continue
000048 |         if re.match(r"^(licen[cs]e|notice|copyright)([._-]|$)", item.name, re.I) or any(p.lower() in ("licenses", "licences") for p in relative.parts[:-1]):

000050 |     for name in explicit:
000051 |         candidate = directory / name
000052 |         try:
000053 |             relative = candidate.relative_to(directory)
000054 |         except ValueError:
000055 |             continue
000056 |         if ".." not in relative.parts and candidate.is_file() and not candidate.is_symlink():
000057 |             candidates.add(candidate)
000058 |     return [(str(file.relative_to(directory)), file.read_bytes()) for file in sorted(candidates)]

000010 | ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
000011 | OUT = ROOT / "packaging/notices"
000012 | TARGET = "aarch64-apple-darwin"
000013 | ROOTS = ["switcher-desktop", "switcher-helper", "switcher-core"]
000014 | 
000015 | 
000016 | def digest(path):
000017 |     return hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
000018 | 
000019 | 
000020 | def run(arguments):
000021 |     return subprocess.run(arguments, cwd=ROOT, env={**os.environ, "RUSTUP_TOOLCHAIN": "stable"},
000022 |                           check=True, capture_output=True, text=True).stdout
000023 | 
000024 | 
000025 | def main():
000026 |     locks = [ROOT / "Cargo.lock", ROOT / "apps/desktop/package-lock.json"]
000027 |     before = {str(p.relative_to(ROOT)): digest(p) for p in locks}
000028 |     inventory = json.loads((OUT / "inventory.json").read_text())
000029 |     assert before == inventory["lock_sha256_after"], "Refresh full inventory for new locks first"
000030 |     corpus = {(p["ecosystem"], p["name"], p["version"]): p for p in inventory["packages"]}
000031 |     result = {"target": TARGET, "cargo_roots": ROOTS, "lock_sha256_before": before,
000032 |               "commands": [], "editions": {}}
000033 |     for edition in ["personal", "public"]:
000034 |         flags = ["--offline", "--locked", "--no-default-features", "--features",
000035 |                  f"switcher-desktop/{edition},switcher-desktop/custom-protocol"]
000036 |         metadata_cmd = ["cargo", "metadata", *flags, "--filter-platform", TARGET, "--format-version", "1"]
000037 |         tree_cmd = ["cargo", "tree", *flags, "--target", TARGET, "--edges", "normal,build",
000038 |                     "--prefix", "none", "--format", "{p}"]
000039 |         for package in ROOTS:
000040 |             tree_cmd.extend(["-p", package])
000041 |         result["commands"].extend([metadata_cmd, tree_cmd])
000042 |         metadata = json.loads(run(metadata_cmd))
000043 |         tree = run(tree_cmd).replace(str(ROOT), ".")
000044 |         (OUT / f"macos-arm64-{edition}-tree.txt").write_text(tree)
000045 |         normal_cmd = tree_cmd.copy()
000046 |         normal_cmd[normal_cmd.index("normal,build")] = "normal"
000047 |         result["commands"].append(normal_cmd)
000048 |         normal_tree = run(normal_cmd).replace(str(ROOT), ".")
000049 |         (OUT / f"macos-arm64-{edition}-normal-tree.txt").write_text(normal_tree)

000051 |         for line in normal_tree.splitlines():
000052 |             match = re.match(r"^(\S+) v(\S+)", line)
000053 |             if match:

000056 |         compact = {"packages": [{k: p[k] for k in ["id", "name", "version", "source", "license"]}
000057 |                                 for p in metadata["packages"]], "resolve": metadata["resolve"]}

000083 |     npm = json.loads(locks[1].read_text())["packages"]

000086 |     def resolve(base, name):
000087 |         parent = PurePosixPath(base)
000088 |         while True:
000089 |             candidate = str(parent / "node_modules" / name)
000090 |             if candidate in npm:
000091 |                 return candidate
000092 |             if parent == PurePosixPath("."):
000093 |                 return None
000094 |             parent = parent.parent
000095 | 
000096 |     def reachable(sections):
000097 |         seen, todo = set(), [("", name) for section in sections for name in npm[""].get(section, {})]
000098 |         while todo:
000099 |             base, name = todo.pop()
000100 |             path = resolve(base, name)
000101 |             if path is None:
000102 |                 unresolved.append({"from": base or "root", "dependency": name})
000103 |                 continue
000104 |             if path in seen:
000105 |                 continue
000106 |             seen.add(path)
000107 |             for section in ["dependencies", "optionalDependencies", "peerDependencies"]:
000108 |                 todo.extend((path, dep) for dep in npm[path].get(section, {}))
000109 |         rows = []
000110 |         for path in sorted(seen):
000111 |             name = path.rsplit("node_modules/", 1)[-1]
000112 |             package = npm[path]
000113 |             row = dict(corpus["npm", name, package["version"]])
000114 |             manifest = ROOT / "apps/desktop" / path / "package.json"
000115 |             row["installed_now"] = manifest.is_file()
000116 |             if manifest.is_file():
000117 |                 local = json.loads(manifest.read_text())
000118 |                 assert local["name"] == name and local["version"] == package["version"]
000119 |             rows.append(row)
000120 |         return rows
000121 | 
000122 |     production = reachable(["dependencies"])
000123 |     build = reachable(["dependencies", "devDependencies"])
000124 |     result["npm"] = {"method": "lock dependency/optional/peer graph; installed manifests version checked; no bundler analysis",
000125 |                      "production_reachable": production, "build_reachable": build,
000126 |                      "unresolved_edges": sorted({json.dumps(x, sort_keys=True) for x in unresolved})}

FILE: current/windows/README.md
SHA256: 75a03d3e26efd3520ebd2a1bc1e9a8794c2f8793b71ad0ea36963c6b0c3e1c44
==============================================================================
000001 | # Claude 切换器 · Windows 版 (Beta)
000002 | 
000003 | > ⚠️ **本版本为 Beta,未经大规模真机测试**。核心逻辑移植自 macOS 稳定版,但 Windows 环境差异大,遇到问题请到 [Issues](https://github.com/517183415-dev/claude-switcher/issues) 反馈。
000004 | 
000005 | ## 系统要求
000006 | 
000007 | - Windows 10 / 11 (64 位)
000008 | - PowerShell 5.1+（Windows 自带,无需安装）
000009 | - [Clash Verge](https://github.com/clash-verge-rev/clash-verge-rev) Windows 版,且 **TUN 模式可用**
000010 | - Claude 桌面版 Windows
000011 | - 安装时需要**管理员权限**一次（注册守护任务 / 锁定时区 / 关闭 IPv6）
000012 | 
000013 | ## 安装
000014 | 
000015 | 1. 先把 Clash Verge 开好（TUN 模式 + 美国节点）——安装器会校验出口必须是美国
000016 | 2. 右键 `install.ps1` →「使用 PowerShell 运行」,或在管理员 PowerShell 中执行
000017 | 3. 如果提示禁止运行脚本,先执行一次:
000018 |    ```powershell
000019 |    Set-ExecutionPolicy -Scope CurrentUser RemoteSigned
000020 |    ```
000021 | 4. 按提示走完,桌面会出现「Claude切换器」快捷方式
000022 | 
000023 | ## 与 macOS 版的差异（注意事项）
000024 | 
000025 | > **注意**：macOS 版已支持多代理客户端（Clash Verge / Shadowrocket / sing-box / ClashX / Surge / Odcloud）与地区自适应（按出口国家自动锁定时区+地区格式，换节点后 `set-region` 一键重配）。Windows 版当前仍默认按美国出口设计，安装时会按 ipinfo 自动锁定时区；其他国家/地区的完整支持在路线图中。另外，自动更新（`update` 命令 + 12 小时自动检查）与 48 小时 IP 审查目前仅 macOS 版提供，后续版本同步。
000026 | 
000027 | | 项目 | 说明 |
000028 | |---|---|
000029 | | 时区名称 | Windows 用 `Eastern Standard Time` 这类 ID,安装器会自动把 IP 城市映射过去 |
000030 | | 保镖频率 | 计划任务登录启动,隐藏窗口常驻,每 10 秒巡检(与 Mac 一致) |
000031 | | IPv6 | 关闭的是所有网卡的 ms_tcpip6 绑定;要恢复: `Enable-NetAdapterBinding -ComponentID ms_tcpip6` |
000032 | | SmartScreen | 首次运行可能弹"Windows 已保护你的电脑" → 点「更多信息」→「仍要运行」 |
000033 | | 账号数据目录 | Windows 版在 `%APPDATA%\Claude`(与 macOS 路径不同,逻辑相同) |
000034 | | 卸载 | 计划任务里删掉 ClaudeBodyguard,删除 `%USERPROFILE%\.claude-switcher` 和桌面快捷方式即可 |
000035 | 
000036 | ## 已知限制
000037 | 
000038 | - 界面是 WPF 重写的第一版,样式以 Mac 版为准对齐,细节可能有出入
000039 | - Clash Verge 的 TUN 开关在 Windows 上如果被更新重置,目前需要手动在设置里重新打开(一键修复会提示)
000040 | - 账号快照切换前请确保 Claude 已完全退出(托盘图标也要退出)

000110 |     $out = Invoke-Core @('checkdetail')
000111 |     $allOk = $true; $matched = 0
000112 |     foreach ($line in ($out -split "`n")) {
000113 |         $kv = $line -split '=', 2
000114 |         if ($kv.Count -ne 2) { continue }
000115 |         $st = $kv[1] -split ':', 2
000116 |         for ($i = 0; $i -lt $rows.Count; $i++) {
000117 |             if ($rows[$i].Name -eq $kv[0] -or ($kv[0] -eq 'TUN网卡' -and $rows[$i].Name -eq 'TUN网卡')) {
000118 |                 $matched++
000119 |                 $d = if ($st.Count -gt 1) { $st[1] } else { '' }
000120 |                 switch ($st[0]) {
000121 |                     'ok'   { $rows[$i] = [pscustomobject]@{ Name = $rows[$i].Name; Detail = $d; Icon = '✓'; Color = '#FF22C55E' } }
000122 |                     'warn' { $rows[$i] = [pscustomobject]@{ Name = $rows[$i].Name; Detail = $d; Icon = '⚠'; Color = '#FFFACC15' } }
000123 |                     default { $rows[$i] = [pscustomobject]@{ Name = $rows[$i].Name; Detail = $d; Icon = '✗'; Color = '#FFF87171' }; $allOk = $false }
000124 |                 }
000125 |                 break
000126 |             }
000127 |         }
000128 |     }
000129 |     $st = Invoke-Core @('status')
000130 |     $win.FindName('CurrentAccount').Text = if ($st -match 'current=B') { 'B' } elseif ($st -match 'current=A') { 'A' } else { '未初始化' }
000131 |     if ($matched -lt $checkNames.Count) {
000132 |         Set-Status '检查未过' ([System.Windows.Media.Color]::FromRgb(0xF8,0x71,0x71))
000133 |         $win.FindName('Banner').Text = "检测输出不完整($matched/$($checkNames.Count))，请点重新检查"
000134 |     } elseif ($allOk) {
000135 |         Set-Status '环境安全' ([System.Windows.Media.Color]::FromRgb(0x22,0xC5,0x5E))
000136 |     } else {
000137 |         Set-Status '检查未过' ([System.Windows.Media.Color]::FromRgb(0xF8,0x71,0x71))

000139 |     }
000140 | }

000001 | # Claude 保镖 (Windows) —— 代理离线时自动关闭 Claude
000002 | # 由安装器注册为计划任务,登录时启动,隐藏窗口常驻
000003 | $ErrorActionPreference = 'SilentlyContinue'
000004 | $LOG = Join-Path $env:USERPROFILE ".claude-switcher\bodyguard.log"
000005 | 
000006 | while ($true) {
000007 |     $coreUp = [bool](Get-Process -Name 'verge-mihomo' -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue)
000008 |     $tunUp  = [bool](Get-NetIPAddress -AddressFamily IPv4 | Where-Object { $_.IPAddress -like '198.18.*' })
000009 |     if (-not ($coreUp -and $tunUp)) {
000010 |         $p = Get-Process -Name 'Claude' -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
000011 |         if ($p) {
000012 |             $p | Stop-Process -Force
000013 |             "$(Get-Date -Format 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss') 代理离线(内核或TUN丢失),已自动关闭 Claude" | Out-File -Append -Encoding utf8 $LOG
000014 |         }
000015 |     }
000016 |     Start-Sleep -Seconds 10
000017 | }

000022 | function Get-Egress {
000023 |     $trace = & curl.exe -s --max-time 8 "https://www.cloudflare.com/cdn-cgi/trace" 2>$null
000024 |     $ip  = (($trace -split "`n" | Where-Object { $_ -like 'ip=*' })  -replace 'ip=','').Trim()
000025 |     $loc = (($trace -split "`n" | Where-Object { $_ -like 'loc=*' }) -replace 'loc=','').Trim()
000026 |     return @{ ip = $ip; loc = $loc }
000027 | }
000028 | 
000029 | # STUN 探针(纯 .NET UDP,手工构造 RFC5389 报文)
000030 | function Get-StunPublicIP([string]$server, [int]$port, [int]$timeoutMs = 2000) {
000031 |     try { $addr = ([System.Net.Dns]::GetHostAddresses($server) | Where-Object { $_.AddressFamily -eq 'InterNetwork' } | Select-Object -First 1) } catch { return $null }
000032 |     if (-not $addr) { return $null }
000033 |     $udp = New-Object System.Net.Sockets.UdpClient
000034 |     $udp.Client.ReceiveTimeout = $timeoutMs
000035 |     try {
000036 |         $txn = New-Object byte[] 12; (New-Object Random).NextBytes($txn)
000037 |         $req = [byte[]](0x00, 0x01, 0x00, 0x00) + [byte[]](0x21, 0x12, 0xA4, 0x42) + $txn
000038 |         $udp.Connect($addr, $port)
000039 |         [void]$udp.Send($req, $req.Length)
000040 |         $remote = New-Object System.Net.IPEndPoint([System.Net.IPAddress]::Any, 0)
000041 |         $resp = $udp.Receive([ref]$remote)
000042 |         $pos = 20
000043 |         while ($pos + 4 -le $resp.Length) {
000044 |             $atype = ([int]$resp[$pos] -shl 8) -bor $resp[$pos + 1]
000045 |             $alen  = ([int]$resp[$pos + 2] -shl 8) -bor $resp[$pos + 3]
000046 |             if (($atype -eq 0x20 -or $atype -eq 0x01) -and $alen -ge 8 -and $resp[$pos + 5] -eq 0x01) {
000047 |                 $ipBytes = @($resp[$pos + 8], $resp[$pos + 9], $resp[$pos + 10], $resp[$pos + 11])
000048 |                 if ($atype -eq 0x20) {
000049 |                     $cookie = [byte[]](0x21, 0x12, 0xA4, 0x42)
000050 |                     for ($i = 0; $i -lt 4; $i++) { $ipBytes[$i] = $ipBytes[$i] -bxor $cookie[$i] }
000051 |                 }
000052 |                 return ($ipBytes -join '.')
000053 |             }
000054 |             $pos += 4 + $alen + ((4 - ($alen % 4)) % 4)
000055 |         }
000056 |     } catch { return $null } finally { $udp.Close() }
000057 |     return $null
000058 | }

000060 | function Test-WebRTC($exitIp) {
000061 |     $servers = @(@("stun.miwifi.com", 3478), @("stun.chat.bilibili.com", 3478), @("stun.l.google.com", 19302), @("stun.cloudflare.com", 3478))
000062 |     $found = @()
000063 |     foreach ($s in $servers) {
000064 |         $r = Get-StunPublicIP $s[0] $s[1]
000065 |         if ($r -and ($found -notcontains $r)) { $found += $r }
000066 |     }
000067 |     if ($found.Count -eq 0) { return @{ code = 'ok'; detail = 'UDP不出网,零暴露' } }
000068 |     $leak = $found | Where-Object { $_ -ne $exitIp }
000069 |     if ($leak) { return @{ code = 'fail'; detail = "暴露非代理IP: $($leak -join ',')" } }
000070 |     return @{ code = 'ok'; detail = '仅暴露代理IP' }
000071 | }

000220 | function Invoke-Switch($target) {

000222 | 
000223 |     # 首次运行:收编当前登录为账号A

000230 |     }
000231 |

000233 | 
000234 |     if (-not (Stop-ClaudeApp)) { "FAIL:Claude 未能退出，请手动退出后重试"; return }
000235 | 
000236 |     if ($cur -ne 'none' -and (Test-Path $DATA)) { Save-Account $cur }
000237 | 
000238 |     if (Test-Path "$BASE\accounts\$target") {
000239 |         Remove-Item $DATA -Recurse -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
000240 |         robocopy "$BASE\accounts\$target" "$DATA" /E /NFL /NDL /NJH /NJS | Out-Null
000241 |         Log "已载入账号$target 存档"
000242 |     } else {

000244 |         New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $DATA | Out-Null
000245 |         Log "账号$target 无存档，将以全新状态初始化"
000246 |     }
000247 |     $target | Out-File -Encoding ascii -NoNewline "$BASE\current.txt"
000248 |     Start-ClaudeApp
000249 |     Log "已切换到账号$target 并启动"
000250 |     "OK:已切换到账号$target"
000251 | }

000258 |     }
000259 |     'status' { Invoke-Status }
000260 |     'fix'    { Invoke-Fix }
000261 |     'switch' { Invoke-Switch $args[1] }
000262 |     default  { "用法: claude-switch.ps1 checkdetail|status|fix|switch A|B" }
000263 | }

000016 | # ---- 1. 出口必须是美国(否则时区会锁错) ----
000017 | $info = try { (& curl.exe -s --max-time 8 "https://ipinfo.io/json" 2>$null | ConvertFrom-Json) } catch { $null }
000018 | if (-not $info -or $info.country -ne 'US') {

000024 | # ---- 2. 自动识别代理组名 ----
000025 | $grp = $null
000026 | $inGroups = $false; $name = $null
000027 | foreach ($line in Get-Content "$CLASH_DIR\clash-verge.yaml" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue) {
000028 |     if ($line -match '^proxy-groups:') { $inGroups = $true; continue }
000029 |     if ($inGroups -and $line -match '^[a-zA-Z]') { $inGroups = $false }
000030 |     if ($inGroups -and $line -match '- name:\s*(.+)') { $name = $Matches[1].Trim('"',"'",' ') }
000031 |     if ($inGroups -and $line -match 'type:\s*select') { $grp = $name; break }
000032 | }
000033 | if (-not $grp) { $grp = Read-Host "未能自动识别代理组名,请打开 Clash Verge 查看后输入" }
000034 | Write-Host "[OK] 代理组: $grp" -ForegroundColor Green
